# TurnOva MVP Progress

**Last updated:** 2026-11-15; main HEAD `d27eec195769d3cd0ea05197b76a8ae1ecd8c67f`. PR #163 native API publish hardening is merged. Sole active PR #164 (`test/release-preflight-strict-validation`) source/test head `35687a394c9a89dd0225be79b66a5be25389f149` passed [CI #1643 / run 43623967660](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/43623967660) **12/12 jobs**: backend .NET 10 594/594 (0 failed/0 skipped), shared Node 189/189, deterministic Python harness 48/48, Rider/Driver Expo, four Android/iOS native builds, native non-container API publish and all four same-run emulator/Simulator smoke jobs. The earlier Driver Android ADB exit-255 smoke failure is addressed with bounded, command-labelled recovery for read-only/idempotent probes; transient recovery and persistent fail-closed fixtures passed. The output-path data-loss review finding is fixed and its thread resolved. This evidence is for the source/test head; the documentation synchronization now being committed requires a new exact-head CI before PR readiness/merge. Reviews are COMMENT-only; no approval is claimed. Strict acceptance remains 0/19 (15 In Progress, 4 Blocked); production readiness is not established.

## 1. Current step

**Current sequential engineering item: #19 RELEASE-01 —** PR #164 implements ten deterministic release-preflight cases (RELEASE-PREFLIGHT-001..010) and four Android smoke transport cases (RELEASE-SMOKE-ANDROID-001..004). Coverage includes strict SemVer/EAS CLI ranges, build-profile boundaries, production API URL/network validation, CLI arguments/reporting, JSON-only stdout, direct/symlink/hardlink configuration-output collision protection, and bounded ADB exit-255 recovery for read-only/idempotent post-launch probes.

- Main HEAD: `d27eec195769d3cd0ea05197b76a8ae1ecd8c67f`. PR #164 branch `test/release-preflight-strict-validation`, source/test head `35687a394c9a89dd0225be79b66a5be25389f149`, base main, Draft, eight changed files, mergeable, one resolved data-loss review thread and zero unresolved inline threads. CI #1643 passed 12/12 on the exact source/test head. The evidence-document synchronization in this commit requires a fresh exact-head CI before readiness/merge. Reviews remain COMMENT-only; no approval is claimed.
- Exact-head [CI #1643 / run 43623967660](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/43623967660) completed **12/12 jobs successfully** on `35687a394c9a89dd0225be79b66a5be25389f149`: backend .NET 10 Release build/tests/publish; shared mobile/auth tests; deterministic Python harness; Rider/Driver Expo validation and production JS bundles; Rider/Driver Android debug and standalone release APKs; Rider/Driver unsigned iOS Simulator apps; and all four same-run Android emulator/iOS Simulator smoke jobs.
- Test evidence: backend **594/594** (0 failed, 0 skipped); shared Node **189/189**; deterministic Python harness **48/48**. Both Android emulator smoke jobs installed/launched their standalone release APKs and reached the login surface; both iOS Simulator smoke jobs reached the role-specific login surface and verified the expected unsigned SecureStore-unavailable banner. These are simulator/emulator results, not signed-device or physical-device acceptance.
- The generalized ADB retry helper is limited to read-only/idempotent probes: package path, activity resolution/stack, UI hierarchy dump/read and logcat. It retries only exit 255, reconnects/waits with bounded timeouts, retains command-labelled diagnostics, and fails closed after three attempts. Install, launch and input side effects are not blindly retried. PID identity, foreground, login, ANR and fatal-exception checks remain strict. Transient/persistent PID-probe, transient-logcat and persistent-activity-stack fixtures passed in the 48-test Python harness.
- Artifacts from CI #1643: native API publish `14124578905`; Rider/Driver Android debug APKs `14125537058` / `14125293349`; Rider/Driver standalone release APKs `14124784541` / `14125048771`; Rider/Driver iOS Simulator apps `14124929431` / `14125643977`; production JS bundles `14125352187` / `14125032649`; Rider/Driver Android smoke `14125723039` / `14125887585`; Rider/Driver iOS smoke `14126148142` / `14126072066`.
- Both release-preflight reports still identify seven external release gates per app. No signed Android AAB/iOS IPA, approved bundle identifiers, EAS linkage, production API URL, signing credentials, physical-device acceptance or store submission is claimed.
- Evidence synchronization across the progress report, acceptance matrix, test plan and Project Brain is complete. Wait for the latest exact-head CI on the current branch tip; only after it passes, recheck reviews/threads and mark PR ready or merge if GitHub permits a normal operation without bypass. After merge, re-read main and proceed with supplemental AI-01 provider-endpoint validation.

## 2. Overall MVP completion

- **Engineering implementation/evidence:** Substantial implementation and deterministic automated evidence exist across the 19 rows; see the row-by-row matrix and test-plan cases. This is not represented as a weighted percentage.
- **Strict acceptance:** **0/19 Verified**, **15 In Progress**, **4 Blocked**. A row is Verified only after every required acceptance item, including external gates, passes.
- **Blocked rows:** DISPATCH-01 requires Product-approved radius and non-overlapping zones; DRIVER-04 and SUPPORT-01 require Product/Operations support-policy decisions; RELEASE-01 requires signed release configuration, physical-device acceptance and store gates.
- **Production readiness:** **Not established.** No live Cosmos, physical-device, signed-release, provider, legal or production-hosting validation is claimed.
- Historical weighted-status scoring is retired and is not used as a proxy for implementation completion.

## 6. Unresolved decisions and blockers

- Internal FIFO role: no customer-facing FIFO ranking or automatic assignment; do not assume whether internal fairness/rotation remains.
- Payment reconciliation: customer dispute/confirmation, the next-ride rule for a non-zero signed delta, and whether a new Union may receive a minimum preceding-ride reconciliation summary after Driver transfer are unresolved.
- Negotiation/booking: the exact negotiation lifetime is unapproved. Production appsettings remains null and booking fails closed until approval; 15 minutes is used only in integration-test fixtures, not as a product default.
- Union operations: legal partnership terms and minimum necessary union oversight/retention require review before production.
- Support: Rider/Driver issue categories, escalation owner and operating process are not approved.
- Infrastructure/device gates: development Cosmos access for transactional-batch/ETag/partition tests; Android/iOS physical-device validation; production provider/hosting/signing configuration.
- No credentials or secrets are stored in this file. Docker/container builds and deployments remain prohibited.

## RIDER-04 direct-payment reconciliation increment (2026-11-07)

- Current sequential item: #16 RIDER-04. #15 DRIVER-04 was inspected and remains Blocked: the PRD leaves Driver support categories, escalation owner and operational process unapproved. No speculative support workflow is added.
- The API accepts one non-negative, at-most-two-decimal amount report only from the assigned Driver after a Completed Cab ride with the current mutually confirmed agreement. Agreed amount/currency are snapshotted from that agreement; delta is agreed minus reported.
- The report is embedded in the existing Rides aggregate and protected by the existing ETag/snapshot comparison. Same-key/same-payload retry is idempotent; conflicting reports cannot overwrite the first report.
- Driver history shows the unverified report. The UnionManager endpoint is filtered by signed unionId claims and returns only ride/Driver references and reconciliation values; it omits Rider identity, route/stops, contacts, exact coordinates and private chat.
- No payment gateway, escrow, customer verification/dispute workflow or delta-based next-ride gate is introduced. The latter two product decisions remain open. Physical Android/iOS entry/history acceptance and live Cosmos ETag/restart validation remain external gates.

- Approved supplemental **CAB-RECON-01 is In progress in PR #70** and remains outside the 19-area denominator; AI-01 is now In progress in the focused sequential branch and remains outside the 19-area denominator.

## PLATFORM-01 ride audit trail increment (2026-11-07)

- Current sequential item: #18 PLATFORM-01. #17 SUPPORT-01 remains Blocked because Rider support categories, escalation owner and operating process are not approved; no speculative support workflow is added.
- The API now stores privacy-minimised `RideAuditEvent` records in the existing `DemoRide` aggregate. The event list is optional for backward compatibility with existing ride documents and is not a new Cosmos container.
- Creation events are written with the initial ride document; subsequent events are appended in the same ETag-protected ride replacement as ride state, agreement version/confirmation or payment reconciliation. The snapshot comparison includes the audit list, and an append-only prefix guard rejects removal, reordering or mutation of prior events.
- Event types cover RideCreated/RideRequested, AgreementProposed, AgreementPartyConfirmed, RideStatusChanged and PaymentReportedUnverified. Events contain ride/Union linkage, actor ID/role, UTC time and relevant state/version/amount/currency/delta; they omit route/stops, exact coordinates, contact data, document references, tokens and private chat.
- Same-key agreement/payment retries and repeated terminal-state calls do not append duplicate events. Deterministic tests cover the full selected-Cab lifecycle, cancellation, Driver decline, System expiry, agreement-confirmation retry, payment-report retry and preservation across a non-audit location update.
- No new container, cross-partition transaction, general-purpose admin audit endpoint or cross-Union visibility policy is added. Live Cosmos multi-instance ETag/restart/retention, production telemetry/alerts/origin configuration and load tests remain external gates.

## RIDER-03 history-triggered expiry reconciliation (2026-11-07)

- Post-PR #94 main audit confirmed the Rider and Driver history endpoints both call the authoritative `FindRideAsync` before returning each history item. Direct ride-read and discovery expiry tests existed, but neither history endpoint had an explicit regression proving that history reads trigger the terminal transition and release the matching Driver reservation.
- The current focused test creates two selected bookings for one Driver in sequence. It expires the first and reads Rider history; after the lease is released, it creates a second booking, expires it and reads Driver history. Both responses must report Expired, each ride must have exactly one System Expired audit event, and the matching lease must be released.

## DRIVER-03 — payslip audit recovery before immutable snapshot (2026-11-07)

- Main after PR #99 is `1863586eebe210eaae78b041f8567f6e9bf85be0`; exact-head CI [42362228646](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42362228646) passed all eight jobs and 251/251 backend tests.
- Audit found that ride status and append-only audit facts are persisted in the `Rides` aggregate, while `Accepted`, `Rejected` and `Completed` work metrics are written separately to `DriverWorkActivities`. If a process stops between those writes, the first immutable monthly snapshot could permanently omit an activity even after a later completion retry.
- PR #100 adds a pre-snapshot reconciliation for selected Cab rides. It replays missing Driver `Accepted` and negotiation-decline events from the authoritative ride audit for the requested month, and repairs missing `Completed` activity/reservation release for completed selected rides. Deterministic activity IDs and create-if-absent writes keep repeated reads idempotent. Existing snapshots remain immutable.
- New API integration regression: `PayslipSnapshotRepairsMissingSelectedRideActivitiesFromAuditBeforeSnapshot` removes the separate activity rows after audit facts exist, including a selected Driver decline from `Negotiating` before any agreement version exists, then verifies the first PDF snapshot reconstructs accepted/completed/rejected counts and remains byte-identical on repeat.
- The Driver app's existing native file/share path uses Expo FileSystem cache and Expo Sharing; Android/iOS physical save/share, accessibility and real PDF-viewer acceptance remain device gates. Live Cosmos cross-container query/create-conflict/restart/ETag validation remains external; this read-repair is not a transactional outbox and does not claim live Cosmos verification.
- Next sequential item after #14: #15 DRIVER-04 — support-category/escalation process is blocked pending Product Owner approval. If still blocked, record the exact dependency and proceed to #16 RIDER-04 payment reconciliation UI/API audit.

The agreement contract and report record carry the actual agreement currency (the existing API integration test includes USD), so the UI could mislabel a valid report.

## Current sequential execution — #12 DRIVER-02 (2026-11-08)

- Main base: `435a62e8dba1c93b630efc7a8e5f02bd93734a94`. Branch: `fix/driver-02-fence-out-of-order-location`.
- Audit found that concurrent foreground GPS callbacks could complete out of capture order. The API previously stamped each accepted update with server receipt time, so a delayed older coordinate could overwrite a newer coordinate and appear fresh to Rider discovery.
- The increment adds optional `LocationCapturedAtUtc` to the Driver profile and `capturedAtUtc` to availability/location requests. The Driver app sends Expo GPS capture timestamps for Online, foreground refresh and watcher updates. The API rejects future timestamps beyond one minute and rejects capture timestamps that do not advance the stored watermark, including both in-zone and zone-exit CAS paths. Server receipt time remains separate for freshness.
- Rider discovery and selected booking (including the post-reservation eligibility reread) now evaluate freshness using capture time, falling back to receipt time for legacy profiles. No product freshness threshold is invented; the configured positive `Dispatch:NearbyLocationMaxAgeSeconds` remains required and missing configuration still fails closed.
- Added API regressions for stale in-zone/out-of-zone GPS updates preserving coordinates, Online state, receipt timestamp and queue membership; stale GPS reuse after Offline; discovery/selected-booking exclusion when server receipt is recent but GPS capture is stale; and future capture timestamps beyond the one-minute clock-skew allowance being rejected without profile/queue mutation. An earlier superseded backend run [42459347697](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42459347697) exposed fixture-only failures (missing Driver fixture initialization and queue seeding); the current test setup explicitly creates the Driver profile and starts the queue assertion from Offline. Latest pre-sync PR tip `d13751865940b13a603d433f45ecd50bc96f191e` has exact-head CI [42466304752](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42466304752): Rider/Driver mobile and shared mobile-auth passed; both Android native builds failed before compilation after three Gradle 8.14.3 distribution-download attempts returned HTTP 504 through the GitHub release redirect; backend passed build but 1 of 317 tests failed (`StartupRejectsMissingJwtConfiguration` for whitespace issuer returned `ObjectDisposedException` instead of the expected validation message); both iOS native builds remain queued. The workflow now configures a 120-second wrapper timeout and retries only transient HTTP 5xx/socket-timeout distribution failures up to three times. A fresh exact-head CI run is required after this documentation-only synchronization. No live Cosmos or physical-device validation is claimed.
- PR #116 was closed as superseded to restore the one-active-feature-PR rule; its Driver availability-recovery helper, six deterministic tests and Driver UI integration have now been integrated into PR #117 alongside the GPS capture-watermark API work. The old branch remains preserved as source history; no code is counted as merged until the combined PR merges.
- **Next:** wait for the current workflow's two iOS jobs to finish; then rerun only the failed Android and backend jobs. If the JWT startup-validation test fails again, inspect and repair the existing test/startup path rather than masking it. Merge #117 only after a fresh exact-head run is fully green, review threads remain resolved and repository policy permits without bypass/self-approval. After merge, reread main and proceed sequentially to #13 TRIP-01 (not DRIVER-03).

- PDF pipeline: SkiaSharp/HarfBuzz draws visible vector glyphs with request-local shaping; PDFsharp 6.2.4 (MIT) appends a low-visibility searchable text layer with embedded Noto Sans SIL OFL 1.1 fonts and ToUnicode. A Skia-only experiment failed 2/340 tests (no embedded font program or searchable long-name text); PDFsharp 6.2.4 is the current replacement and must pass fresh exact-head CI.

A 409 is treated as an idempotent retry only after canonical same-partition read-back verifies event ID, event type, zone/service partition, actor, trace, reason and no offer ID. Missing or mismatched canonical data fails closed. The writer preserves the existing `QueueEntries` partition and adds no container/provider.

## RELEASE-01 deterministic emulator harness increment

The Android emulator script now has standard-library tests with a fake SDK/AVD manager/emulator/ADB. The harness sets a non-default `ANDROID_AVD_HOME`, verifies the named AVD files are created in that exact directory, exercises install/activity launch/process-stability/report output, and rejects malformed package identifiers before SDK access. It does not replace the actual emulator smoke. CI runs all `scripts/tests/test_*.py` cases; exact-head results are pending.

### RELEASE-01 PID-scoped crash-marker regression

The first fake-SDK run exposed a second smoke-script defect: awk returned success for an empty/no-match logcat because the PID-scoped expression had no explicit END status. The script now sets a match flag and exits nonzero when no target-process marker is found. The fake ADB fixture emits an unrelated-process React Native marker in the passing path and a target-PID marker in a negative path; both outcomes are asserted. Fresh exact-head CI is required.

## RELEASE-01 login UI smoke increment

The Android smoke now requires the fresh-install login title, email/password hints and sign-in action in the UI hierarchy after launch. The iOS Simulator smoke uses the local Apple Vision framework to recognize the same text in its captured screenshot. Neither test enters credentials or calls an API/authentication provider. These checks distinguish a rendered login surface from a merely surviving process; they remain simulator-only evidence.


### RELEASE-01 fake ADB UI fixture correction

The first run after adding Android UI hierarchy assertions failed only in the fake-SDK unit test because the mock ADB dispatcher did not implement the new `uiautomator dump` and `cat` calls. The fixture now returns a deterministic hierarchy containing the login title, email/password hints and sign-in action, and the test asserts those nodes and the report flag. The real emulator job remains required to verify the platform command and actual rendered UI.

## RELEASE-01 wrapped OCR banner regression (2026-11-09)

- Review found that Apple Vision may return a visually wrapped banner as multiple OCR lines. Matching the forbidden phrase against newline-joined text could miss a phrase split at a line boundary.
- The iOS OCR checker now normalizes whitespace before both required-login and forbidden-banner checks. The fake Swift fixture emits `Unable to restore your` and `session...` on separate lines, and the harness requires the smoke to fail.
- This is a test-harness correctness refinement only; no app runtime, authentication, API or production configuration changes. PR #141 exact-head CI must rerun all 12 jobs on its updated head before merge.


## RELEASE-01 SecureStore Keychain simulator signing (2026-11-09)

- The first clean-state iOS smoke still rendered `Unable to restore session` after `simctl erase`; the screenshot and Vision text confirm this is not stale data left by a previous simulator session.
- The native iOS CI build explicitly uses `CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` and `CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO`. Expo SecureStore reads the iOS Keychain during app startup; an unsigned app has no application/keychain access entitlement, so the simulator cannot exercise the same storage path as a signed app. The app's generic restore error is retained; no runtime workaround was added.
- PR #141 keeps the unsigned simulator artifact for compile/identity evidence, then ad-hoc signs an ephemeral copy with a bundle-scoped `application-identifier` and `keychain-access-groups` entitlement before install. The signature is simulator-test-only, uses no Apple certificate/provisioning profile/production secret, and is checked before launch. The clean-state screenshot/OCR must still prove the banner is absent; if it remains, investigate SecureStore further rather than weakening the assertion.


### RELEASE-01 cold simulator first-boot timeout correction (2026-11-09)

- On PR #141 head `1cdb8276fb0af71bb12ba68e3a05d47ec8ab4bc3`, run [#42628714272](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42628714272) completed the backend/mobile/native build and both Android emulator jobs successfully. The Driver iOS smoke failed before install: `simctl bootstatus` remained in first-boot Data Migration/LaunchServicesMigrator and returned nonzero after approximately 2m20s. The Rider iOS job remained in progress when this attempt was superseded; no fresh iOS login screenshot was produced by the failed Driver job.
- Do not pass a made-up timeout flag to `simctl`. The script now bounds the actual `xcrun simctl bootstatus <UDID> -b` process with Python `subprocess.run(timeout=300)`. `TURNOVA_SIMULATOR_BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` is validated to 1–600 seconds; the default is five minutes to allow cold migration while remaining bounded.
- The fake-SDK harness verifies the exact bootstatus arguments, fails closed on a nonzero bootstatus result, and uses a sleeping fake command plus a 1-second timeout to prove timeout returns 124 before app install or success report. No app runtime, authentication, API, Cosmos, production signing or deployment code changed.
- Latest PR #141 head is `dc5d21327a843d46ed507603e5eed605af1b3f6a`; exact-head CI [#1035 / 42633933073](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42633933073) is pending. Merge remains prohibited until every required job passes on the final head, iOS screenshots show the required login surface without stale-session/storage banners, and repository policy permits.

## Latest sequential handoff — AUTH-02 epoch-fenced token persistence (2026-11-10)

Supplemental AI-01 PR [#142](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/142) merged at `6f0dc4821ae6408c7057f6b34aa4990ea01d948d` after documentation-head CI [#1113 / 42774588830](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42774588830) completed all 12 jobs successfully. The current sequential feature is AUTH-02 PR [#143](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/143), branch `fix/auth-02-refresh-logout-epoch-race`, code head `97ffdbb0819dab51046980698035cd364fce3bb2`. Shared `saveSessionTokensIfCurrent` serializes token-pair writes and checks the captured epoch before writing and after each secure key; stale partial pairs are cleared before the storage queue releases. Rider and Driver advance the epoch at sign-in start, logout and refresh-expiry cleanup, detach the previous single-flight refresh, and fence stale login/logout/expiry completions after asynchronous storage/cache work. The new `isAuthenticatedSessionCurrent` guard binds in-memory tokens to the epoch that actually completed secure persistence. Protected API calls and Driver payslip downloads are rejected immediately after logout/account-switch/expiry advances the epoch, even while old tokens remain in React state. A restored session binds only after the stored pair and authoritative profile are validated.

AUTH-LOGIN-031–035 cover guarded pair persistence, logout/login races, stale completion and single-flight detachment; AUTH-LOGIN-036–038 cover the authenticated-epoch request guard. Existing AUTH-LOGIN-027 storage serialization coverage remains. The previous code head passed CI #1120 12/12; require fresh exact-head backend, shared tests, Rider/Driver typechecks and bundles, native Android/iOS builds, four simulator smokes and native non-container API publish before merge.

## Latest sequential handoff — AUTH-02 authenticated-epoch request fencing (2026-11-10)

PR [#143](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/143) is the sole active feature PR. Branch: `fix/auth-02-refresh-logout-epoch-race`. Current code/test head: `97ffdbb0819dab51046980698035cd364fce3bb2`. PR #144 was closed as a superseded narrower duplicate; #143 retains the broader stale-completion and single-flight protections and now includes the request-fencing correction.

New shared helper `isAuthenticatedSessionCurrent(sessionEpochRef, authenticatedEpochRef)` fails closed unless the token-bearing session epoch equals the active epoch. Rider and Driver bind that authenticated epoch only after guarded token persistence or successful startup restore. Their protected API wrappers reject requests before network dispatch when logout, account switch or session expiry has advanced the active epoch but old tokens remain in component state. Driver payslip download applies the same guard. Successful logout/expiry cleanup clears the authenticated epoch; failed secure deletion leaves the old epoch mismatched, so it cannot authorize new requests.

Three deterministic tests cover matching/mismatched epochs, immediate invalidation when the epoch advances, and missing/invalid epoch values. Test-plan cases AUTH-LOGIN-036–038 were added. This is local client-side concurrency evidence only; it does not validate TriSend server-side refresh-token reuse semantics or signed-device SecureStore.

Preceding code head `dc5daf17517beaf32e9a5a398932116047545c6a` passed all 12/12 jobs in [CI #1120 / 42782377492](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42782377492): backend 373/373, shared JavaScript 109/109, Python native-artifact tests, Rider/Driver validation and bundles, four native builds and four simulator smokes. ### AUTH-02 realtime session invalidation follow-up (2026-11-10)

The final review found that the authenticated-epoch guard blocked new REST requests after logout/account-switch/expiry, but existing SignalR transports could remain connected until asynchronous SecureStore and payslip-cache cleanup completed. Their token factories could also fall back to an old in-memory token, and open event handlers were guarded only by component mount state. The follow-up adds a shared connection-stop registry, stops active Rider/Driver realtime transports immediately when the session epoch advances, gates every SignalR token read by the captured authenticated epoch before and after SecureStore access, and guards Rider location/status/agreement plus Driver offer/selection/agreement/status handlers and reconnect recovery. Protected API helpers recheck the epoch after response-body parsing. Deterministic helper tests and AUTH-LOGIN-039–042 cover stale token reads, captured epochs, connection teardown and late response rejection. AUTH-02 remains In Progress pending fresh exact-head CI, live TriSend rotation/reuse semantics and signed-device SecureStore/Keychain acceptance.


### AUTH-02 session-resource invalidation follow-up (2026-11-10)

The final security review extended the epoch fence beyond REST and SignalR event callbacks. The active Driver foreground location watcher is now registered with the session-resource stop registry and is stopped immediately on logout/account switch/refresh expiry; permission and watch-start continuations recheck the captured authenticated epoch before starting or publishing location. Rider and Driver asynchronous history/agreement loaders also recheck the current authenticated epoch before applying results. `startSignalRConnection` now checks its owner again after a successful `connection.start()`; if invalidation happened while start was pending, it stops the newly-started transport and reports inactive. `readJsonResponseIfCurrent` provides a deterministic deferred-body guard used by both protected API helpers and Driver payslip error parsing. AUTH-LOGIN-039–044 cover stale SignalR token reads, connection registry teardown, captured epochs, late response bodies, foreground location stop and in-flight SignalR start. Fresh exact-head CI and same-run simulator artifacts are required before merge.


## Latest sequential handoff — AUTH-02 startup-restore fencing (2026-11-10)

The active PR #143 remains the only feature PR. A final concurrency audit found startup restore was not bound to the session epoch. If a Rider/Driver began a replacement sign-in while the old /auth/me or /auth/refresh request was pending, the old restore could later publish its profile/tokens or clear the new token pair. The shared restore helper now accepts a captured-epoch predicate, checks it after each asynchronous boundary, persists rotated tokens only through saveSessionTokensIfCurrent, and clears only the exact token pair still owned by a current restore. Partial-pair cleanup is serialized and re-reads the pair before deleting, so a stale read cannot erase a newer login. Rider and Driver startup effects fence restore state, errors and loading completion; a successful restore adopts the epoch it creates.

Four deterministic tests AUTH-LOGIN-045–048 cover delayed startup profile response, delayed refresh result, stale 400/401 cleanup and partial-pair cleanup racing a replacement login. This work is in the existing PR #143; no second feature PR was created. Current branch head before the new commit was cc24c22e0ef686b700ce3c513f21133db30418e4; the next exact-head CI must be inspected on the new commit. AUTH-02 remains In Progress pending live TriSend rotation/reuse and signed-device SecureStore/Keychain acceptance.


## Latest sequential handoff — AUTH-02 startup-restore race closure (2026-11-10)

The active PR #143 now binds startup restore to the captured session epoch. The restore helper checks its predicate after token reads, initial /auth/me, /auth/refresh, guarded token persistence and the final /auth/me. Stale 400/401 cleanup re-reads and clears only the exact pair still owned by the restore. Partial-pair cleanup is serialized with token writes and compares the current pair to the original partial snapshot before deleting. Rider and Driver mount effects fence profile/tokens, error and loading state. Four tests AUTH-LOGIN-045–048 cover delayed profile, delayed refresh, stale rejection and partial-pair snapshot races; AUTH-LOGIN-049 covers final profile lookup after token rotation racing a replacement login. Current code head 1919aeffe5c8321585f935add6298a4d3fefce2d is under exact-head CI #1142 / 42796355621. Live TriSend rotation/reuse and signed-device SecureStore/Keychain acceptance remain external.

## Latest execution handoff — AUTH-02 SignalR token refresh and RELEASE-01 simulator timeout (2026-11-10)

PR [#143](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/143) remains the sole active feature PR. Latest code commit: [33326106419fdbaf628b598782ff63fa59067ce6](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/commit/33326106419fdbaf628b598782ff63fa59067ce6).

The Rider selected-ride SignalR connection and all three Driver SignalR connections now use a shared expiry-aware token factory. A finite future JWT exp returns the stored token without an extra REST request. Expired or locally unverifiable tokens call the existing epoch-guarded apiFetch('/api/v1/auth/me') path; that path owns 401 refresh, single-flight coordination and session invalidation. The helper re-reads SecureStore and checks the captured epoch after every asynchronous boundary. It passes an empty fallback to the SecureStore reader so a missing stored token cannot silently fall back to an obsolete in-memory token. Seven deterministic shared tests AUTH-LOGIN-050–056 cover finite/invalid/overflow expiry, valid-token no-refresh, expired-token rotation, missing-exp server validation, logout during validation/read and absent-token fail-closed behaviour. Expected shared suite count is 136/136 (previous 129 + 7); exact-head CI remains pending.

## Latest AUTH-02 session-state privacy increment — 2026-11-10

Source/test commit `b9f02acdc0d67ca5ecf70d4796ef40f933a90677` adds role-specific session reset helpers used by both apps. The audit found that Rider refresh-expiry cleanup cleared tokens/user but left trip history, discovery, agreements, route and help state in memory; Driver cleanup left some queue, membership, onboarding, vehicle/document and selfie fields. A replacement login could therefore render stale prior-account data while the new profile loaded.

The Rider reset clears tokens/user, active ride/history, discovery/selection, agreements, route/stops/passengers, trip-intent/help state and map-follow state, then resets the selected-booking attempt key/location. The Driver reset clears tokens/user, queue/membership/availability, active ride/history, agreements/payment inputs, onboarding/Union assignment, vehicle/document references, selfie data and help state. Both resets preflight all setter callbacks before mutating state and allocate fresh collection instances.

AUTH-LOGIN-057 and AUTH-LOGIN-058 plus two additional shared helper regressions bring the shared JavaScript suite from 136 to 140 tests. The reset runs before replacement login, after refresh expiry, and after successful logout (after secure token deletion). It does not invent backend logout/revocation semantics or claim secure-storage deletion succeeded when it failed.

Exact-head CI [#1151 / 42831865877](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42831865877) is pending on the source/test head; documentation synchronization changes the PR head and requires its own fresh exact-head run. CI #1150 / 42819171585 is still in progress on the superseded documentation-only head after a transient Rider iOS artifact-upload DNS failure and targeted retry; do not use it as final evidence.

## Latest sequential handoff — RIDER-03 single-active Rider booking (2026-11-11)

Main HEAD at branch creation: `ab988b02b01d89394e5213586f9ee24f0e78c6b1` (PR #145 merged). Active engineering branch: `feat/rider-03-single-active-booking`. Draft PR [#146](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/146) is open at exact head `7e49cce79698fe5c107d8d8014e5eca61dd54d56`; no merge has occurred. CI [#1199 / 42904689967](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/42904689967) is pending.

Backend:
- `RiderActiveBookingReservation` is stored as ID `active-selected-booking` in the existing `RideIdempotency` container partitioned by `/riderId`.
- `CosmosRiderActiveBookingReservationStore` uses same-partition transactional batches for create + append-only audit, ETag-conditional permanent promotion + audit, and ETag-conditional matching release + audit.
- The in-memory implementation uses atomic `ConcurrentDictionary` updates and an injectable `TimeProvider` for deterministic expiry tests.
- The selected-booking route acquires the Rider reservation before the per-Driver reservation and ride create. Same-key/same-request retries may continue the matching reservation; a different active RideId is rejected with HTTP 409.
- `HasActiveRiderBookingAsync` reconciles terminal rides and expired temporary orphans against the ride aggregate. The store never steals an expired reservation on timestamp alone; permanent or active reservations fail closed.
- Driver acceptance promotes the Rider reservation to permanent. If an existing pre-deployment selected ride lacks the Rider record, the acceptance path may repair it only when the Rider partition is otherwise free and the request fingerprint reconstructed from the ride matches.
- Terminal cancellation, Driver decline, expiry and completion release only the matching RiderId+RideId. Terminal read-repair retries an interrupted release.
- The Rider app loads active ride history immediately after authentication, moves to Activity, disables Book, and guards discovery/submission while the server-owned ride status is active.

New backend store tests: `RiderActiveBookingReservationStoreTests` covers atomic different-Ride acquisition, same-Ride/hash replay, Rider partition independence, permanent promotion, exact/stale release, expired-lease no-steal and audit privacy. API integration tests cover one Rider/two Drivers with different keys, independent Riders, cancellation/rebooking, accepted/permanent replay, completion release and expired-negotiation reconciliation. Existing Driver-decline coverage now asserts Rider reservation release. Shared mobile tests cover active statuses and the Rider app guards.

No live Cosmos claim is made: CI exercises the in-memory path. Live Cosmos ETag/transactional-batch/multi-instance/restart behavior and Android/iOS physical-device acceptance remain external. Production negotiation lifetime remains unset and selected booking fails closed with 503 until Product approves/configures it.

Next after this PR merges: re-read `main`, continue sequentially to #6 DISPATCH-01 audit, and implement any remaining independent in-scope API/mobile/persistence/test gaps before moving to DISPATCH-02. Maintain one feature PR at a time.

Strict acceptance remains **0/19** (15 In Progress, 4 Blocked); this is a complete-evidence count, not an implementation-completion percentage. Production readiness is not established.

## PLATFORM-01 deterministic Operations Union-audit recovery — current handoff (2026-11-12)

- Base main: `4817351616d6f0d7f0c9a51a0863cb92ce4caea4`; PR #155 merged after exact-head CI #1358 passed 12/12 on `72836c5ec4497663f5c700af7cd8d211259e1212` (backend 500/500, shared mobile 179/179, Python 46/46 and all native/smoke jobs).
- Branch: `fix/platform-01-union-audit-idempotent-recovery`. Code adds a versioned hashed event identity over Driver/current Union/selfie capture timestamp/reason; identical retries preserve `ProfilePhotoReviewedAtUtc`; a repeated request restores the deterministic audit after a profile-success/audit-failure interruption. The audit writer verifies every canonical field after an in-memory duplicate or Cosmos 409 point-read using the exact event ID and Driver partition, retries a briefly missing read up to three times and fails closed on mismatched or persistently missing data. Actor attribution uses opaque JWT `sub` or `Operations`, not email-like `Identity.Name`.
- Added deterministic tests for identity stability/privacy, actor resolution, matching/missing/mismatched canonical reads, bounded retries and cancellation, plus an API integration regression that removes the audit after profile commit and verifies retry restores the same event ID/timestamp without duplication. Cases PLATFORM-AUDIT-021–027 are recorded in the test plan. This branch's CI is not yet run.
- Driver profile and Union audit use separate Cosmos containers; no cross-container transaction is claimed. Live Cosmos 409/ETag/partition/multi-instance/restart/retention validation, telemetry export/alerts, production CORS, load and physical-device acceptance remain external.

## PLATFORM-01 deterministic assignment-audit recovery — 2026-11-13

- PR [#156](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/156), current source/test head `bec115c9a7f706a3e22fcccb2da24c2d0bc011f0`. The DriverUnionAssignment record now stores a private last-assignment audit snapshot: versioned deterministic event ID, event type, validated original actor, previous Union ID/name and occurrence timestamp. Assignment reason and destination remain the canonical assignment fields.
- `DriverUnionAssignmentAuditSnapshot.CreateExpectedEvent` rejects incomplete, unsafe-actor or hash-mismatched snapshots; legacy assignments with no snapshot return no expected event and are not synthesized. `EnsureUnionAssignmentAuditAsync` point-reads the exact Driver/event ID first, verifies every canonical field including actor, and creates only when missing; a concurrent duplicate/409 uses bounded canonical read-back.
- The transfer route repairs the previous assignment event before replacing its snapshot. After the new assignment upsert, the audit event is created from the stored snapshot. A same active target+reason retry repairs a missing event and returns the original assignment idempotently. Ops history reads also repair the current assignment event. Driver `/api/v1/driver/union` strips event ID/type/actor/previous-Union/timestamp snapshot fields.
- Added PLATFORM-AUDIT-039–042 and tests for deterministic initial/transfer IDs, no PII in IDs, legacy no-invention, unsafe/corrupt snapshots, same-target/reason retry, missing-audit recovery on transfer retry/history read, and Driver response redaction.
- CI #1416 on superseded parent `953a3e780c4410cd6cb03538482639404907d287` passed backend **537/537**, shared mobile **179/179** and Python **46/46**. Native Android/iOS and downstream smoke jobs were cancelled after later commits. CI #1418 is queued for source/test head `bec115c9...`; no result is attributed to that head until its run completes. Final documentation-head CI remains required.
- No live Cosmos ETag/409/partition/multi-instance/restart/retention or cross-container atomicity is claimed. Strict acceptance remains **0/19** (15 In Progress, 4 Blocked); production readiness is not established.
